### [1] [Curvature-driven spin transport in rank-two string-Carroll hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01634)
*Nikko John Leo Lobos,Reggie Pantig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了无挠背景下探针物质的规范应力-自旋Ward系统的弦-卡拉罗(rank-two)极限，系统保留了Riemann-自旋耦合力、第二纵向动量投影及仅在二维纵向核下允许的纵向boost二重矢；通过线性展开自旋幅ς并按近视界参数λ=ε²展开，避免高阶自旋热反馈污染；确定p=0为唯一使诱导应力与曲率源在首阶Ward阶平衡的有限混合应力扇区；在非极端静态球对称外视界正则分支上，纵向与横向自旋幅满足相同稀释律，曲率力始于O(ςλ²)；对一般等熵态方程，定态系统约化为一个热力学四次积分与一个局域反演；对仿射常声速族p=αℰ+Π，给出显式参量化解、两自旋通道的领头轮廓，以及远离声速点的焓、压强与快度的定半径线性自旋修正；响应由纵向Ricci标量ℛ⁽ᴸ⁾_h与纵向自旋流主导；证明定态闭球Killing流在正则伪规范改进下不变；在RN黑洞中，该响应于|Q|/M=2√2/3处消失；在EMD族中，零点为R₋/R₊=(1+a²)/2；Frenkel极限消除此响应及纵向boost自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建更精细的 Carroll 极限以容纳非平凡自旋动力学，超越传统 rank-one Carroll 自旋流体力学，刻画强引力场（如黑洞视界附近）中具有内禀自旋的探针物质的非平衡响应，并厘清自旋-曲率耦合、纵向boost自由度与热力学约束之间的关系。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法：首先提取Ward系统中关于独立自旋振幅ς的线性项，再以近视界小参数λ=ε²进行双重展开，严格排除O(ς²)热力学自旋反馈；在光滑非极端静态球对称外视界正则分支上实施局域归一化；对barotropic态方程施行定态约化；对affine常声速族p=αℰ+Π求解耦合微分系统，获得显式参量化解；利用Killing矢量构造守恒流并分析其伪规范不变性；在RN与EMD背景中解析求解响应零点。

Result: 1) rank-two string-Carroll极限下存在非零Riemann-自旋力与纵向boost二重矢；2) p=0是唯一保证首阶Ward一致性（应力-曲率源平衡）的扇区；3) 纵向与横向自旋幅在视界附近服从相同稀释律，曲率力始于O(ςλ²)；4) affine常声速族存在闭式解，给出自旋修正的焓、压强、快度剖面；5) 响应由ℛ⁽ᴸ⁾_h=(f₂+3f₁ψ₁)/2与纵向自旋流决定；6) 闭球Killing流在正则伪规范下守恒；7) RN与EMD中响应存在可精确计算的零点；8) Frenkel极限完全抹除该响应及boost自由度。

Conclusion: rank-two string-Carroll框架是描述黑洞视界附近自旋探针物质非平衡动力学的必要且最小扩展，其物理响应受纵向Ricci标量与自旋流协同调控，且具有深刻的几何（伪规范不变性）与热力学（p=0扇区唯一性）约束；Frenkel极限过度简化，丢失关键自旋-时空耦合自由度。

Abstract: We derive the rank-two string-Carroll limit of the canonical stress--spin Ward system for probe matter on a fixed torsion-free background. Beyond rank-one Carroll spin hydrodynamics, this retains the coupled Riemann--spin force, a second longitudinal momentum projection, and the longitudinal boost bivector allowed only by a two-dimensional longitudinal kernel. We first extract the term linear in an independent spin amplitude $ς$ and then expand in the near-horizon parameter $λ=ε^2$, avoiding contamination by the omitted $\mathcal{O}(ς^2)$ thermodynamic spin feedback. For finite mixed stress and $S^{λμν}=\mathcal{O}(ςε^p)$, $p=0$ is the unique sector in which the induced stress and curvature source balance at the first generic Ward order. On the regular branch of a smooth nonextremal static spherical outer horizon, locally normalized longitudinal and transverse spin amplitudes obey the same dilution law, while the curvature force first appears at $\mathcal{O}(ςλ^2)$. For a general barotrope, the stationary system reduces to one thermodynamic quadrature and one local inverse. For the affine constant-sound-speed family $p=α\mathcal E+Π$, $0\leqα<1$, we obtain an explicit closed-form parametric solution, leading profiles for both spin channels, and fixed-radius, linear-spin corrections to enthalpy, pressure, and rapidity away from the sonic point. The response is governed by $\mathcal R^{(L)}_h=(f_2+3f_1ψ_1)/2$ and the longitudinal spin flux. We prove that the stationary closed-sphere Killing flux is invariant under regular pseudo-gauge improvements. In Reissner--Nordström, the response vanishes at the radial-tidal inversion $|Q|/M=2\sqrt{2}/3$; in the Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton family, its zero is $R_-/R_+=(1+a^2)/2$ for $0\leq a<1$. The Frenkel limit removes this response and the longitudinal boost sector.

</details>


### [2] [A Unified Treatment of the Self-Force Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01644)
*Beka Modrekiladze,Ira Z. Rothstein,Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于世界线有效场论，构建了适用于任意非零应力-能量环境的引力自作用形式体系，通过闭合时间路径积分导出普适有效作用量，可系统计算致密天体运动方程至任意阶；主导阶运动方程由环境应力-能量张量的推迟两点函数统一表征；该框架能同时处理耗散（动力摩擦）与保守力，并在尘埃、无粘流体和相干场中验证了结果；文中指出Chandrasekhar“库仑对数”实为耗散in-in作用量中UV发散导致的重整化群对数，且在牛顿极限下对该类环境与轨迹具普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种普适、相对论协变的引力自作用计算框架，以处理致密天体在含非零应力-能量张量的复杂环境中所受自力，超越传统真空或弱场近似限制。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline EFT）结合闭合时间路径（closed-time-path, CTP）积分方法，构造环境依赖的有效作用量；利用应力-能量张量的推迟两点函数提取主导阶运动方程；通过动量空间谱分解与UV发散分析揭示‘库仑对数’的RG本质。

Result: 获得普适的引力自作用有效作用量，导出以环境T_{μν}的 retarded two-point function 表征的主导阶运动方程；成功复现尘埃、无粘流体及相干场中的动力摩擦力；证明Chandrasekhar对数是耗散in-in作用量中UV发散引发的RG对数，且在牛顿极限下对广义环境与轨迹普适。

Conclusion: 世界线CTP-EFT提供了一种系统、协变、可重整化的引力自作用计算范式；‘库仑对数’并非唯象参数，而是耗散动力学中尚未被探索的RG流的首项对数效应，标志着引力耗散现象具有深层量子场论结构。

Abstract: This paper introduces formalism, based on worldline effective field theory, which allows one to systematically calculate the gravitational self-force on a compact object immersed in an environment with non-vanishing stress energy. Using the closed time path integral we present a universal effective action that can be used to calculate the equations of motion of a compact object due to its interaction with the environment to any order in the relevant expansion parameters, the relative importance of which depends upon the choice of environment. We show that the leading order equations of motion can be universally written in terms of the retarded two-point function of the stress-energy tensor for the environment. The resulting action can be used to calculate both dissipative (dynamical friction) and conservative forces in a completely relativistic fashion. We demonstrate the utility of the result by calculating the dissipative force due to dust, an inviscid fluid, and a coherent field. Our results agree with those previously derived in the literature. We furthermore show that the famous ``Coulomb Log" found by Chandrasekhar should be interpreted as a renormalization group log due to a UV divergence that renormalizes the dissipative part of the in-in action. We then prove that this log is universal in the Newtonian limit in that its value is universal, for a generic class of environments and trajectories. This log is the leading log in an RG flow in the dissipative action that has yet to be explored.

</details>

*M. Ilyas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了缓慢旋转的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（BEC）暗星，通过联合求解TOV、Hartle一阶转动及Postnikov-Hinderer潮汐形变方程，采用含Lee-Huang-Yang（LHY）修正（强度ζ）的多方物态；发现ζ从0到1.5连续变化时，平均场到修正物态的过渡平滑；(m, a_s)参数扫描表明无法同时满足2 M⊙最大质量与GW170817类潮汐形变约束Λ₁.₄ ≲ 800；I-Love关系在12个BEC模型中普适性达0.13%；不同ζ序列对称偏离主曲线；该求解器对核物质（SLy, APR4）、MIT袋模型夸克物质及双流体混合星均表现稳健；跨物态类（核、混合、BEC暗星）的I-Love序列可统一至~5%精度内，而自束缚夸克星显著偏离（达90%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建自洽的BEC暗星理论模型，检验其是否能同时满足致密星观测约束（如最大质量、潮汐形变），并探究I-Love等普适关系在超越标准核物质物态（含量子修正、暗成分、夸克自由度）下的稳健性。

Method: 联合数值求解广义相对论恒星结构方程：Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（静力学平衡）、Hartle一阶转动理论（轴对称慢转）、Postnikov-Hinderer潮汐形变方程（Love数k₂）；物态采用含LHY量子修正的BEC多方模型P ∝ ρ^{1+1/n} + ζ ρ^{5/3}；系统扫描参数(m, a_s, ζ)，并与SLy、APR4核物质、MIT袋模型及双流体混合星对比。

Result: ① LHY修正强度ζ∈[0,1.5]导致物态平滑过渡，无相变或隐藏结构；② 不存在(m,a_s,ζ)使BEC暗星同时满足M_max≥2 M⊙且Λ₁.₄≲800；③ I-Love关系在BEC模型中偏差≤0.13%，ζ=0与ζ=1序列呈系统性对称偏移；④ 求解器复现核物态文献结果（误差<1%），正确给出夸克星M-R趋势；⑤ 双流体混合星M_max非单调依赖中心暗物质分数；⑥ 核/混合/BEC星I-Love序列统一至~5%，而自束缚夸克星最大偏离达90%。

Conclusion: BEC暗星虽具理论吸引力，但当前LHY修正物态框架无法协调关键多信使约束；I-Love普适关系在包含量子修正、暗成分及核子自由度的致密星模型中高度稳健（≤5%），但对自束缚夸克星失效，表明其起源与核子强相互作用及中子星壳层结构密切相关。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating Bose--Einstein condensate (BEC) dark stars by solving the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff, Hartle dipole, and Postnikov--Hinderer equations together for a polytropic equation of state with a Lee--Huang--Yang correction of strength $ζ$~\citep{Panotopoulos2026}. A continuous scan of $ζ$ from 0 to 1.5 shows the mean-field-to-corrected transition is smooth, with no hidden structure at intermediate values. Scanning the underlying boson parameters $(m,a_s)$ more broadly, we find that a $2\,M_\odot$ maximum-mass bound and a GW170817-like tidal bound $Λ_{1.4}\lesssim800$ cannot be satisfied simultaneously anywhere in this equation-of-state class. The $I$-Love universal relation holds to $0.13\%$ across twelve $(m,a_s,ζ)$ models, and the $ζ=0$ and $ζ=1$ sequences sit on opposite sides of the master curve by a consistent, non-random offset. Applied without modification to realistic nuclear matter (SLy, APR4), the same solver reproduces published maximum masses to within $1\%$; applied to self-bound MIT-bag quark matter it gives the expected mass--radius shape; and once extended to a two-fluid baryon-plus-dark-matter formalism, it shows that the maximum mass of a hybrid star is not a monotonic function of the central dark-matter fraction. Pooling $I$-Love sequences across nuclear, hybrid, and BEC dark-star models, we find they collapse onto a single curve to within about $5\%$, while self-bound quark stars sit far off it, departing by up to $90\%$.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称度规平行 $f(Q)$ 引力中幂律形式 $f(Q)=\beta Q^{m+1}$ 的晚期暗能量动力学，利用宇宙时钟（CC）、DESI DR2 BAO 及三组 Ia 型超新星（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）联合约束模型；结果表明该模型在统计上优于 $\Lambda$CDM（AIC 支持），EoS 显示类精质子行为，减速参数为负，加速转变红移 $z_t \in [0.680, 0.745]$，Statefinder 轨迹渐近趋于 $\Lambda$CDM 固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晚期宇宙学观测（如 DESI DR2 BAO、宇宙时钟、SNe Ia）精度与互补性提升，为在不依赖背景假设前提下检验修改引力理论（特别是对称 teleparallel $f(Q)$ 引力）提供了新契机；旨在通过数据驱动方式检验 $f(Q)$ 模型对宇宙加速膨胀的描述能力。

Method: 采用对称 teleparallel 几何框架下的幂律 $f(Q) = \beta Q^{m+1}$ 模型，以有效状态方程参数 $w_{\rm eff}(z)$ 刻画宇宙演化；联合拟合宇宙时钟（CC）、DESI DR2 重子声波振荡（BAO）及三组独立 Ia 型超新星数据（Pantheon+、DES-SN5YR、Union3）；使用 $\chi^2$ 最小化、AIC/BIC 模型比较、EoS 重构、减速参数 $q(z)$、高阶宇宙学参量、Statefinder 对角线（$\{r,s\}$）及 $Om(z)$ 诊断进行系统分析。

Conclusion: 幂律对称 teleparallel $f(Q)$ 引力模型能自洽、竞争性地描述晚期宇宙加速，其动力学等效于弱耦合精质子场，且在观测精度不断提升背景下展现出与 $\Lambda$CDM 相当甚至更优的统计表现，是修改引力中一个有前景的替代理论。

Abstract: The increasing precision and complementarity of late-time cosmological observations provide new opportunities to test modified gravity against the observed expansion history of the universe. The combination of DESI DR2 BAO measurements with cosmic chronometers (CC) and independent Type Ia supernova compilations provides a data-driven test of cosmic acceleration. Motivated by this progress, we investigate late-time dark energy dynamics in symmetric teleparallel $f(Q)$ gravity by adopting the power-law form $f(Q)=βQ^{m+1}$ and characterizing cosmic evolution through the effective equation of state (EoS) parameter. The model is constrained using CC, DESI DR2 BAO and three independent Type Ia supernova compilations, namely Pantheon+, DES-SN5YR and Union3. The inferred Hubble constant values are mutually consistent, yielding $H_0=67.60^{+1.60}_{-1.66}$, $67.08^{+1.66}_{-1.61}$ and $68.16^{+1.46}_{-1.43}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, respectively. Statistically, the proposed $f(Q)$ model yields lower $χ^2_{\min}$ values and is favored by the AIC for all dataset combinations, whereas the BIC favors $Λ$CDM for the CC + DESI DR2 + Pantheon+ and CC + DESI DR2 + DES-SN5YR combinations and indicates no meaningful preference between the two models for the CC + DESI DR2 + Union3 dataset combinations. The reconstructed EoS indicates quintessence-like behavior, while the negative deceleration parameter confirms the current accelerated expansion. The transition from deceleration to acceleration occurs within $0.680\leq z_t\leq0.745$. Higher-order cosmographic parameters and the Statefinder and $Om(z)$ diagnostics consistently support this quintessence-like behavior, with the Statefinder trajectories approaching the $Λ$CDM fixed point in the far future. Overall, the power-law $f(Q)$ model provides a viable and competitive description of late-time cosmic acceleration.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用AdS/CFT对应下的黑洞热力学框架，结合非线性电动力学（磁荷版本）构造含PFDM与暗能量场的黑洞解；计算热力学量（温度、熵、压强、Gibbs自由能），分析P–V图与Clapeyron方程；运用几何热力学（GTD）构建Ruppeiner/Quevedo度规，提取热力学曲率及其奇点结构；对比临界指数与已知流体及宇宙学系统。

Result: 1) SBH/LBH一级相变仅由非线性磁项触发；2) PFDM参数λ增大完全抑制相变并消除正则核心；3) 暗能量在w > −1（quintessence）时增强相变强度（增大Gibbs面积与潜热、缩小临界区），在w < −1（phantom）时导致无相共存的spinodal失稳；4) GTD曲率标度律为|R| ∝ |t|^−1，与范德瓦尔斯流体、de Sitter视界等一致。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) and a dark-energy field on the phase structure of a nonlinear magnetically charged Anti--de Sitter (AdS) black hole. We find that the small--large black hole (SBH/LBH) phase transition is driven by the nonlinear magnetic sector, while the dark sector does not generate additional transitions. In particular, increasing the magnitude of the PFDM parameter $λ$ suppresses this phase transition, eventually driving the system toward a single-phase regime. Moreover, a nonzero $λ$ prevents the formation of a regular black hole core. The effect of the dark-energy field depends on its equation-of-state parameter: in the quintessence regime, increasing its intensity strengthens the first-order phase transition, increasing the Gibbs construction area and latent heat while reducing the critical regime; in the phantom regime, the system exhibits spinodal behavior without phase coexistence. Finally, geometrothermodynamics (GTD) shows that the curvature singularities of the thermodynamic equilibrium manifold reproduce the phase structure of the black hole, while the associated power-law scaling near criticality is consistent with previous results for black holes, cosmological horizons, and real fluids, suggesting a universal thermodynamic behavior across these systems.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the geodesic structure and thermodynamics of a $κ$-deformed Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter black hole in a Kiselev quintessence field, where dynamics are governed by a mass-dependent lapse deformation. We resolve this by either deriving a modified entropy or rescaling the mass, both of which restore the first law and yield a consistent Smarr relation. Abstract: Building on Jacobson's thermodynamics derivation of Einstein field equations, the "generalized entropy and varying-G" (GEVAG) framework was proposed to consistently incorporate generalized horizon entropy $S=f(A)/4G$ into both the thermodynamics and the geometry of spacetime. A related "entropy-gravity correspondence" (EGC) instead is equivalent to promoting this horizon relation to a radially varying bulk coupling $G_\text{eff}(r)$. We clarify the similarities and fundamental distinctions between these approaches. Although they share the same horizon consistency relation, their global causal structures and asymptotic geometries can differ substantially. In addition, the radial prescription of EGC introduces an additional ambiguity for multi-center configurations. We further examine a near-horizon extension of GEVAG, which restores the equivalence between thermodynamic and ADM masses that is absent in the horizon-only formulation. We then discuss the implications of these different prescriptions for the Bekenstein bound. The Bekenstein bound provides a surprising link between $G_\text{eff}$ in near horizon GEVAG, EGC, and the one obtained in the recent $f(R)$-reconstruction approach of generalized entropy geometry via Wald entropy. Finally, we raise the most important question: what exactly is the $G$ measured in laboratories?

Abstract: The study of the dynamics of regular black holes is an emergent area of research. Recent works have discussed how to deform the Einstein field equations to regularize spherically symmetric black hole solutions due to the introduction of a new physics scale acting as regulator. In this exploratory paper, we consider for the first time the problem of constructing field equations in which the regularization scale is a dynamical field. Such field equations may provide an effective description of the evolution of the cores of regular black holes due to the backreaction of matter, and a novel avenue for constructing alternative dynamical solutions of theoretical and phenomenological interest. We discuss a general family of field equations describing spherically symmetric gravity coupled to a scalar field, and study whether the sector with a constant scalar field contains solutions describing static regular black holes with no additional physics scales aside from the ones provided by the scalar field. Due to the specific structure of the D_5 symmetry, the vacuum expectation values (vevs) satisfy particular relations such that, for both quark models, the up- and down-quark mass squared matrices (i.e., M_d M_d^\dagger and M_u M_u^\dagger) can be simultaneously block-diagonalized by the same unitary transformation, forcing the CKM matrix to be block-diagonal and thus yielding vanishing mixing angles and CP violation. Considering the latest experimental constraints on the muon anomalous MDM, the new interactions in the FDM can make significant contributions to the LFV processes $l_{j}^{-}\to l_{i}^{-}γ$ and $l_{j}^{-}\to l_{i}^{-}l_{i}^{-}l_{i}^{+}$, the predicted branching ratios can well reach the future experimental sensitivity.

Abstract: The lack of signals in direct detection experiments has placed the canonical Weakly Interacting Massive Particle (WIMP) paradigm under severe tension, motivating a shift toward the sub-GeV Light Dark Matter (LDM) regime. However, realizing detectable LDM interaction rates typically requires large couplings to the visible sector, which leads to a severe thermal underabundance of the dark matter relic density within standard cosmology. We instead introduce a finite-radius, time-smeared version of the Bondi mass and adjoin this operator to the matter and graviton observables in an arbitrarily small asymptotic neighborhood of the cut. We separately analyze spacetimes with and without black holes and find, in both cases, Type III$_1$ von Neumann algebras that satisfy non-trivial nesting relations. In Minkowski spacetime, the resulting algebra reconstructs the spacelike wedge associated with the cut. In a stationary black hole spacetime, it reconstructs the region bounded by the cut and the black hole bifurcation surface, providing an asymptotically flat analogue of an entanglement wedge. In the limit as the cut is moved to past infinity, we recover a Type I$_{\infty}$ algebra for spacetimes without black holes and a Type II$_{\infty}$ algebra for black hole spacetimes. For algebras at finite cuts of retarded time, we construct a Type II$_\infty$ regularization of the black hole algebra whose renormalized von Neumann entropy agrees with the generalized entropy. In appendices, we prove two technical results about quantum fields, in a Schwarzschild spacetime, that asymptote to the Minkowski vacuum at infinity: a split property for unbounded, spacelike separated regions and the construction of a faithful, normal, and semifinite Hartle-Hawking weight whose modular flow is Schwarzschild time evolution.

Abstract: A central challenge in diagnosing quantum chaos is to distinguish genuine many-body scrambling from kinematic effects of the spectrum. Krylov state complexity, or spread complexity, has emerged as a powerful diagnostic, with its characteristic growth, peak, and relaxation often taken as signatures of chaos. Result: 获得具有参数尺度分离的AdS₂真空族：IIB中由无界F₅与H₇通量控制，IIA中由无界F₄、F₈与H₇通量控制；大通量极限下弦耦合gₛ→0、所有内部半径Rᵢ ≫ ℓₛ、KK能隙远高于AdS₂曲率；存在N=(1,1)二维超对称分支；该构造规避了针对扩展超对称（如N≥2）的尺度分离no-go定理。

Conclusion: 首次实现几何上尺度分离的AdS₂通量真空，为AdS₂/CFT₁全息对偶提供了可控强耦合边界场论的弦论实现基础，并揭示了有限超对称性（N=(1,1)）对突破尺度分离限制的关键作用。

Abstract: Motivated by the question of whether the internal scales of a compactification can be parametrically decoupled from the external curvature scale, and by the possibility of probing such vacua holographically, we construct the first AdS$_2$ flux vacua with parametric scale separation. We compactify type II string theory on a $G_2$ structure orbifold times a circle in the presence of smeared spacetime-filling O1/O5-planes or O4/O8-planes, together with NSNS and RR fluxes. We derive the two-dimensional dilaton-gravity effective theory and exhibit families of vacua in which unbounded $F_5$ and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIB, while unbounded $F_4$, $F_8$, and $H_7$ fluxes provide parametric control in type IIA. For large flux quanta, the string coupling becomes parametrically weak, all internal bulk radii become parametrically large in string units, and the Kaluza--Klein scale separates from the $\mathrm{AdS}_2$ curvature scale. We analyze the ten-dimensional Killing-spinor equations and identify parametric branches preserving $\mathcal N=(1,1)$ supersymmetry in two dimensions. We explain how our geometrically scale-separated AdS$_2$ vacua are compatible with a recent no-go theorem for scale separation in theories with extended supersymmetry. Finally, we discuss various T-dualities leading to other type IIB and type IIA compactifications on $G_2$ structure spaces times a circle and $SU(3)$ structure spaces times a two-torus.

The exponent is fixed by the dimension and by nothing else in the Ansoldi/geometric comparison; the on-shell two-point amplitude of Erbin et al. and JT gravity agree with its $D=4$ member in the senses made precise below. After an extensive literature review, the author believes that this observation and constructed area measure is novel. The area measure will, however, emerge as the natural candidate for the extension of the earlier non-extended body path space measures to two dimensional simplicial complexes. We further show that the area measure of this paper is exactly the law of the area of a triangle, two of whose sides are drawn from the measure obtained in the companion manuscripts for the one-dimensional Polyakov integral. Abstract: We investigate how a fractional oscillator reacts to external forces when the dynamic law includes a damping term that is explicitly fractional. The corresponding three-parametric fractional differential equation (in the Caputo sense) admits exact solution. Two of the fractional parameters are associated with the intrinsic dissipation mechanism that produces continuous dissipation of energy. The third fractional parameter characterizes the damping term against which the oscillator is driven by the external force. When external time-dependent forces are included, the fractional dynamic law is associated with the Newton-Scott-Blair model of viscoelastic materials. The Laplace transform of the external force defines the profile of the solution; specific examples include the absence of external forces as well as constant, sinusoidal and stepped external forces. With a constant driving force, the system reaches a fixed position after a transient period. With a sinusoidal driving force, the system exhibits persistent oscillatory behavior. These results suggest that the external driving force determines the long-term behavior of the fractionally damped oscillator.

Abstract: Metadynamics is a class of enhanced-sampling methods that is widely used in molecular modeling. Here, we consider metadynamics with a one-dimensional collective variable and explore the limitations of using Gaussian hills in light of existing convergence results. We reduce the corresponding evolution problem to a replicator-type differential equation and analyze its long-time behavior. For a periodic collective variable, we prove the convergence of metadynamics. However, the situation is fundamentally different when the collective variable is defined on a finite interval. In this case, the stationary solution only exists in the weak sense as a finite atomic measure. The solution to the differential equation weakly converges to it. Consequently, metadynamics is ineffective due to the absence of a clear quasi-stationary state. A quasi-stationary, transient solution can only be captured in the "INTERVAL" framework if the free energy outside the finite interval remains nearly constant across sufficiently large regions compared to $σ$.

Abstract: We define the notion of superintegrability of a Hamiltonian system on a stratified symplectic space. Abstract: We study the three-boson Schrodinger operator on the two-dimensional integer lattice with pairwise contact interactions. First, we obtain precise asymptotics of the two bound states below the essential spectrum at zero total quasimomentum in the strong-coupling limit mu to infinity: the ground state energy is -3 mu + 6 + O(1/mu), and the first excited state energy is - mu + C + O(1/mu), where C = 4 - delta approximately 3.96458, with delta determined by the transcendental equation b0(delta) = 1/(1+delta) involving the lattice Green's function. The associated spectral gap is 2 mu + O(1). Second, using the discrete Agmon comparison method and the Paley-Wiener theorem, we establish exponential localization of the ground-state wavefunction with a logarithmic upper bound on the decay rate alpha(mu) at most ln(3 mu) + O(1/mu), reflecting the bounded nature of the lattice dispersion; the sharp asymptotic rate is conjectured. Third, at quasimomentum pi, the parity symmetry is preserved, but the odd quadratic form becomes positive definite with a unique eigenvalue of order 1/mu that never reaches the Birman-Schwinger threshold, so the odd subspace yields only a virtual level. The even subspace supports exactly one bound state with energy -2 mu + 6 + 8/mu + O(1/mu^2), and the spectral gap to the essential spectrum is mu - 2 - 8/mu + O(1/mu^2). The reduction from two bound states at zero to one at pi preserves the total spectral flow and is a lattice-specific phenomenon. Our approach uses an invariant subspace decomposition of the Birman-Schwinger operator and the Krein-Rutman theorem to guarantee uniqueness and strict positivity of the ground state. These results have direct implications for quantum simulation of few-boson systems in optical lattices.

Abstract: This paper presents the closed-form Eshelby's tensor for an arbitrarily oriented polyhedral inclusion in a bimaterial domain under general uniform eigenstrain. The formulae provide the complete elastic and thermoelastic bimaterial Eshelby's tensors, which are verified against analytical solutions for spherical and cuboidal inclusions parallel to the bimaterial interface, and finite element results of an inclined cuboid. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临界慢化（critical slowing down）作为 tipping point 的早期预警指标，在随机、多稳态或亚稳态系统中缺乏普适性，亟需一个基于动力系统谱理论的严格、广义框架。

Method: 基于Kolmogorov生成元的Ruelle–Pollicott谱分析，针对斜椭圆Itô扩散过程，建立不变统计量对RP谱块的敏感性分解；引入残差（residue）与谱分母的乘积结构；结合Doob h-transform构造Q-过程、逃逸时钟与加权通向概率，处理亚稳态系统；定义非可逆性指标N(f)刻画残差主导响应。

Result: 证明早期预警并非仅由RP谱隙闭合（gap closure）导致，残差增长可独立引发经典信号（如方差发散、红化）；给出N(f)>1作为残差驱动放大的严格判据；在fold分岔中精确导出内势阱恢复时间~(ε_c−ε)^{1/2}与跨势阱转移率~(ε_c−ε)^{3/2}的渐近标度，区分分岔诱导与噪声诱导突变；Doob漂移关联最优Girsanov重要性采样。

Conclusion: 早期预警是RP谱块、可观测量和扰动方向三者耦合的属性；残差而非谱隙主导响应行为；该谱分解框架统一处理随机性、非正规性、非可逆性与亚稳态，超越传统线性稳定性范式。

Abstract: Tipping points---abrupt, potentially irreversible reorganizations of a system's statistical state---are commonly anticipated through critical slowing down: recovery slows, autocorrelation and variance rise, and spectra redden. This paradigm is powerful near simple equilibrium bifurcations but is not a general theory for stochastic, multistable, or metastable systems. We develop such a theory from the Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) spectrum of Kolmogorov generators of hypoelliptic Itô diffusions. Resolving the sensitivity of invariant statistics on RP spectral blocks shows that each contribution factorizes into a spectral denominator and a residue coupling the block to both the observable and perturbation direction. Small denominators permit large responses; residues produce them. Thus a closing RP gap is not sufficient for an early warning, while growing residues can generate the classical signature with no gap closure. An early-warning signal is therefore a property of a triple: RP block, observable, and perturbation direction. We demonstrate this on a stochastic non-normal system whose RP spectrum is exactly frozen, yet classical indicators become more alarming than during genuine gap closure. The RP decomposition attributes this to residue growth and yields an index $\mathcal{N}(f)$ satisfying $\mathcal{N}(f)\leq 1$ for reversible dynamics; hence $\mathcal{N}(f)>1$ certifies residue-driven amplification. For metastable systems, one killed problem yields two complementary spectral objects: the Doob $Q$-process isolates in-well recovery, while escape clocks and committor-weighted destination probabilities govern interwell transitions. In a one-dimensional fold, these scale as $(ε_c-ε)^{1/2}$ and $(ε_c-ε)^{3/2}$, separating bifurcation-induced from noise-induced tipping. Doob drift also connects to optimal Girsanov sampling.

</details>


### [80] [Asymptotic long-range order for the XY-model on random geometric graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02618)
*Margherita Disertori,Max Mihailescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机几何图上定义的XY模型，证明了在低温极限下存在淬火意义下的长程有序，并通过凸性论证与Brascamp–Lieb不等式建立两点函数的非零下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序介质中连续对称自旋系统的相变行为，特别是如何在随机几何图这类非规则图上实现经典XY模型的长程有序。

Method: 采用淬火分析方法，结合随机几何图的渐近正则性估计、凸性论证以及Brascamp–Lieb不等式控制自旋关联函数。

Result: 在β→∞且依赖于n和ε的标度下，XY模型在G_{n,ε}上以概率趋近于1地表现出长程有序，即两点函数具有一致非零下界。

Conclusion: 即使在随机环境（随机几何图）中，只要其几何结构在大尺度下足够规则，经典XY模型仍可在淬火意义上实现低温长程有序，这扩展了Mermin–Wagner定理在无序系统中的适用边界。

Abstract: We study the classical $XY$-model on random geometric graphs $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$, which are obtained by sampling $n \in \mathbb{N}$ independent points in a finite domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^d$, $d \geq 2$, and connecting two points by and edge if their distance is of order $\varepsilon > 0$. We refer to $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ as the random environment. Letting $\varepsilon \to 0$ as $n \to \infty$ at a sufficiently slow rate, these graphs capture the geometry of $Ω$.
  Denoting the inverse temperature by $β$, we show that in the limit $β\to \infty$ at a rate depending on $n$ and $\varepsilon$, the $XY$-model on $\mathcal{G}_{n, \varepsilon}$ exhibits long range order in the sense that we prove a lower bound away from zero on the two-point function. Our result is quenched in the random environment: long-range order holds with large probability, converging to one as $n \to \infty$. To prove the statement, we show that with high probability the environment is sufficiently regular to apply a convexity argument and the Brascamp--Lieb inequality.

</details>

### [81] [Smeared spectral functions from lattice QCD: opportunities and challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02239)
*Shoji Hashimoto*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了从欧几里得关联函数重构或约束“抹平谱函数”（smeared spectral functions）的最新方法，强调其在强子真空极化、τ衰变、半轻子B/D衰变及稀有衰变等关键物理观测量中的应用，并指出该反问题本质上病态，难以获得模型无关且不确定性可控的可靠结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多关键高能物理观测量（如muon g-2的强子真空极化贡献、τ衰变、B/D半轻子衰变、B→K(*)ℓ⁺ℓ⁻等）可表达为抹平谱函数的积分；而谱函数无法直接从格点QCD等欧几里得计算中获取，需通过病态反演重建。

Method: 基于欧几里得关联函数（Euclidean correlators）及其衍生输入，采用各类正则化反演方法（如贝叶斯方法、最大熵法、Tikhonov正则化、机器学习辅助拟合等）重构或约束抹平谱函数。

Result: 系统梳理了近年在谱函数反演方面的进展，确认了抹平可观测量对减小系统误差的优势，但尚未建立普适、严格、模型无关且具量化不确定性的重构框架。

Conclusion: 抹平谱可观测量为连接格点QCD与实验提供了重要桥梁，但其反演问题本质病态，亟需发展兼具数学严谨性、物理可解释性与不确定性传播能力的新方法。

Abstract: A broad class of physical observables can be expressed in terms of smeared spectral functions. Important examples include the hadronic vacuum polarization contribution to the muon $g-2$, hadronic $τ$ decays, inclusive semileptonic $B$ and $D$ decays, and amplitudes for rare processes such as $B\to K^{(*)}\ell^+\ell^-$. In recent years, a variety of methods have been proposed to reconstruct or constrain such spectral functions from Euclidean correlators and related inputs. However, the inverse problem is intrinsically ill-posed, making it difficult to obtain fully reliable, model-independent results with controlled uncertainties. In this contribution, I summarize recent developments, discuss the opportunities offered by smeared spectral observables, and highlight the main challenges that remain.

</details>


### [82] [Probing Criticality Using GMM-Based Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02522)
*Shashank Sharma,Dipankar Chakrabarti,Vipul Arora*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的标量势，兼具自旋模型的高效可采样性与标量场理论的径向自由度，从而支持对自发对称破缺（如Higgs机制）和临界现象的研究；验证了其在ℤ₂、U(1)规范及无序系统中能复现对应普适类的关键临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋模型虽易于热浴更新，但因约束而缺失径向涨落，无法描述自发对称破缺；标量理论虽具完整自由度，但采样效率低。亟需兼具高效采样与物理完备性的新模型。

Method: 构造具有目标对称性（ℤ₂、U(1)规范、无序）的高斯混合模型（GMM）标量势，通过控制成分权重与协方差结构，使有效势在长程行为上匹配目标连续场论的重整化群流，确保普适类一致性；结合蒙特卡洛模拟验证临界指数与标度律。

Result: 成功构建ℤ₂对称GMM标量模型，在2D数值模拟中精确复现Ising普适类的临界温度、磁化率标度与关联长度发散行为；推广至U(1)规范场与无序系统，证明其通用框架可行性。

Conclusion: GMM标量势提供了一种新的、可高效采样的连续场论离散化方案，在保持临界物理真实性的同时突破了传统自旋/标量模型的采样-物理权衡困境，为格点场论、量子模拟及机器学习增强的统计物理建模开辟新路径。

Abstract: Spin models with a given symmetry are easier to sample than scalar theories with the same symmetry on a lattice, as the constrained nature of spin variables enables cheap heat-bath updates. However, this constraint suppresses radial fluctuations, and consequently, spin models cannot be used to study the phenomena of spontaneous symmetry breaking, such as the Higgs phenomenon. To address this, we propose a class of scalar potentials based on Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) that are as easy to sample as spin models with a given symmetry. These potentials can be designed to belong to the same universality class as the theory of interest, thereby reproducing its critical properties while enabling efficient sampling. We construct such models for global $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry, $U(1)$ gauge symmetry, and disordered systems. We also verify by numerical experiment that the case of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry in two dimensions lies in the two-dimensional Ising universality class.

</details>


### [83] [Double Parton Distributions in the nucleon: Lattice parameter dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02752)
*Daniel Reitinger,Christian Zimmermann,Andreas Schäfer*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了双部分子分布（DPDs）在格点QCD中的计算，重点分析了有限体积效应、格点间距依赖性及夸克质量参数依赖性，使用CLS合作组生成的Wilson-Clover费米子系综。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）对超出标准模型（BSM）物理的探测能力，需更精确理解标准模型背景，尤其是难以实验分离的双部分子相互作用（DPIs）；而DPIs由双部分子分布（DPDs）参数化，其格点计算具有重要理论与实验价值。

Method: 采用CLS合作组提供的Wilson-Clover费米子格点规范场系综，系统研究DPDs计算中有限体积效应、格点间距（a）依赖性以及夸克质量（特别是pion质量）依赖性。

Result: 量化了有限体积、格点分辨率及夸克质量对格点提取DPDs的影响，为未来物理点（physical point）下DPDs的外推与误差控制提供了关键基准。

Conclusion: DPDs的格点计算必须系统控制离散化误差、有限体积效应与夸克质量外推偏差；本工作为实现物理点下可靠DPDs预言奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: To make full use of the HL-LHC, especially in the search for BSM physics, all Standard Model backgrounds have to be understood with increased precision. This is especially difficult for Double Parton Interactions (DPIs) and more generally Multi Parton Interactions (MPIs), which are very difficult to isolate precisely in experiment. DPIs are parameterized by Double Parton Distributions (DPDs) and the lattice determination of the latter is, therefore, especially attractive. In our previous work on DPDs on the lattice, we focused on the results obtained from one gauge ensemble with higher than physical pion mass. In this work, we extend our efforts by investigating artifacts introduced by finite volume and the dependence on the lattice spacing. Moreover, we consider the dependence on the mass parameters. This study is carried out by employing the Wilson-Clover fermion gauge ensembles generated by the CLS collaboration.

</details>


### [84] [On the equivalence of left-hand-cut and three-body formalisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02840)
*André Baião-Raposo,Raul Briceño,Sebastian M. Dawid*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文证明了两种处理格点QCD中左截断问题的方法——三体方法与两体左截断形式论——在解析和数值上等价，并在标量模型中验证了二者均能正确复现无穷体积粒子-束缚态散射振幅及对应的左截断；但指出二者忽略的指数压制有限体积效应在小格点体积下可能显著，影响振幅提取精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中因交换奇点导致的标准有限体积谱-散射振幅关系失效的‘左截断问题’，并厘清近期提出的多种方案之间的理论一致性。

Method: 通过解析推导建立[JHEP 06 (2024) 051]三体方法与[JHEP 08 (2024) 075]两体左截断形式论的严格等价性，并在含S波两粒子束缚态与单粒子交换的标量场模型中进行数值检验。

Result: 证实两种方法在无限体积散射振幅和有限体积谱中左截断结构上完全等价；同时发现二者共同忽略的指数压制有限体积修正项在小体积下数值显著，构成系统性误差源。

Conclusion: 三体与两体左截断形式论本质统一，但实际格点计算中必须纳入被忽略的指数小项以保障振幅提取的可靠性，尤其在物理体积较小时。

Abstract: The "left-hand cut problem" in lattice QCD arises when exchange singularities invalidate standard relations used to extract scattering amplitudes from the finite-volume spectrum. In recent years, several approaches were proposed to resolve this problem. We establish analytically and verify numerically the equivalence between the three-body approach proposed in [JHEP 06 (2024) 051] and the two-body left-hand cut formalism developed in [JHEP 08 (2024) 075]. We test the equivalence in a scalar model describing a particle scattering from an $S$-wave two-particle bound state through one-particle exchange. Both approaches reproduce the same infinite-volume particle--bound-state amplitude and correctly encode the associated left-hand cut in the finite-volume spectrum. Crucially, we identify that the exponentially suppressed finite-volume effects neglected in both formalisms can be numerically large, compromising amplitude extraction at small lattice volumes.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验首次搜索D_s*+介子的重子衰变D_s*+ → p n̄，在4.128–4.226 GeV能区7.33 fb⁻¹数据中未发现显著信号，给出分支比上限为1.3×10⁻⁴（90% CL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲介子强衰变中的重子数守恒与QCD因子化机制，探索非轻子重子衰变中可能的新物理或非微扰效应。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，利用能量-动量守恒约束与粒子鉴别（dE/dx、TOF、EMC）进行事例筛选，并采用侧带拟合与蒙特卡洛模拟确定背景与效率，最终通过CLs方法计算分支比上限。

Result: 未观测到D_s*+ → p n̄显著信号；在90%置信水平下，分支比上限为ℬ(D_s*+ → p n̄) < 1.3 × 10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 该衰变过程极稀有，当前精度下未偏离标准模型预期；结果为后续高统计量实验及理论模型（如重夸克有效理论、SU(3)_F破缺估计）提供了关键约束。

Abstract: The first search for the baryonic decay $ D_{s}^{*+} \to \ p \bar{n} $ is performed using $e^+e^-$ collision data taken at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV, collected by the BESIII experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$. No significant signal is observed, and an upper limit on the branching fraction is set to be $1.3\times 10^{-4}$ at the $90\%$ confidence level.

</details>

Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到ψ(3686)→pK⁻Kₛ⁰Ξ̄⁰+c.c.衰变，统计显著性达11.5σ，测得分支比为(2.84±0.40±0.25)×10⁻⁶，拓展了多奇异粲偶素衰变的实验认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索罕见的多奇异强子末态在粲偶素衰变中的产生机制，检验QCD因子化、SU(3)味对称性破缺及重子产生动力学模型。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的(2.712±0.014)×10⁹个ψ(3686)事例，采用多变量拟合与组合背景抑制技术，在质心系不变质量谱中提取信号，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟校准探测效率与系统误差。

Result: 首次确立ψ(3686)→pK⁻Kₛ⁰Ξ̄⁰+c.c.衰变的存在，测量分支比为(2.84±0.40±0.25)×10⁻⁶，统计显著性11.5σ。

Conclusion: 该结果为ψ(3686)→B̄B（B为含两个或更多s夸克的重子）类衰变提供了首个实验基准，约束了非微扰QCD中重子碎裂函数与手征夸克模型参数。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2.712 \pm 0.014) \times 10^{9}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the decay of $ψ(3686)\to p K^- K_S^0 \bar Ξ^0+c.c.$ is observed for the first time with a statistical significance of $11.5σ$. The branching fraction of this decay is measured to be $(2.84\pm 0.40\pm 0.25) \times 10^{-6}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This measurement extends the experimental information on rare multi-strange $ψ(3686)$ decays and provides an experimental reference for future studies of related decay modes.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验2015–2018年6 fb⁻¹的13 TeV pp碰撞数据，通过B_s^0 → D_s^- π^+衰变中的干涉效应，对CPT与洛伦兹对称性破缺进行高精度检验；在SME框架下对Δa_μ的灵敏度达O(10⁻¹⁴) GeV量级，并给出B_s^0系统中CPT破坏参数z的最精确测量：Re(z) = 0.018 ± 0.016 ± 0.003，Im(z) = 0.003 ± 0.004 ± 0.002。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPT与洛伦兹对称性是量子场论基本支柱，其微小破缺可能揭示量子引力或新物理；B_s^0混合系统对这些破缺极为敏感，是理想的探针。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器采集的B_s^0 → D_s^- π^+衰变事例，提取时间依赖衰变率干涉项，拟合CPT-violating参数z（定义为|q/p|−1+i arg(q/p)），并在标准模型扩展（SME）框架下将观测效应映射至背景场分量Δa_μ。

Result: 首次在B_s^0 → D_s^- π^+道中实现对z的直接测量：Re(z) = 0.018 ± 0.016 ± 0.003，Im(z) = 0.003 ± 0.004 ± 0.002；对应SME中时空各向异性系数Δa_μ的约束达O(10⁻¹⁴) GeV。

Conclusion: 未发现CPT或洛伦兹对称性破缺的显著证据；所获z值与零相容，且为B_s^0系统迄今最严格CPT检验；结果对量子引力模型、SME参数空间构成强约束。

Abstract: Tests of fundamental symmetries, such as $C\!PT$ and Lorentz invariance, provide sensitive probes of physics beyond the standard quantum-field-theory framework. Possible violations of these symmetries are investigated through interference effects in decays of $B_s^0$ mesons to $D_s^-π^{+}$ states. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected with the LHCb detector in 2015--2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of $13~\mathrm{TeV}$, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$. The results are interpreted within the Standard Model Extension framework, yielding sensitivities to the Lorentz-violating parameter $Δa_μ$ at the ${\cal O}(10^{-14})\,\mathrm{GeV}$ level. Without assuming Lorentz-invariance violation, the $C\!PT$-violating parameter $z$ in the $B_s^0$ system is measured to be $\mathcal{R}e(z) = 0.018 \pm 0.016 \pm 0.003$ and $\mathcal{I}m(z) = 0.003 \pm 0.004 \pm 0.002$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. These results represent the most precise constraints on $C\!PT$ violation in the $B_s^0$ system.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的约100.87亿个J/ψ事例，通过J/ψ→ΛΛ̄衰变产生的超子作为有效束流，结合束流管材料作为靶，首次观测到反超子-质子非弹性散射过程Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p（3.1σ），并测量了Λp→Σ⁰p与Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p的截面，分别为(4.7±0.7) mb和(1.1±0.3) mb，揭示了超子-核子与反超子-核子相互作用的明显不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超子-核子（YN）及反超子-核子（ȲN）相互作用是强子物理与致密星物态方程的关键问题；现有理论模型对YN/ȲN非弹性道（特别是Σ⁰p末态）缺乏精确约束，且尚未有ȲN→baryonic final state的实验观测。

Method: 采用J/ψ→ΛΛ̄产生极化超子束流，利用束流管铍（⁹Be）与氢（来自油）成分分别作为靶，通过末态粒子动量重建与不变质量分析提取Λp→Σ⁰p与Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p截面；对⁹Be靶数据应用表面散射假设外推至自由质子靶，并与氢靶直接测量结果交叉检验。

Result: 在p_Λ = 1.074 GeV/c下：Λ+⁹Be→Σ⁰+p+⁸Li截面为(10.1±1.4±0.7) mb，Λ̄+⁹Be→Σ̄⁰+p+⁸Li为(1.7±0.6±0.4) mb；氢靶直接测得Λp→Σ⁰p为(3.2±1.1±0.5) mb，Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p为(1.5±0.5±0.1) mb；组合截面为(4.7±0.7) mb与(1.1±0.3) mb；Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p信号显著度达3.1σ，系首例反超子非弹性散射含重子末态的实验证据。

Conclusion: 实验首次确立Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p过程，给出迄今最精确的Λp→Σ⁰p与Λ̄p→Σ̄⁰p截面值，揭示YN与ȲN相互作用存在显著手征/重子数不对称性，为发展基于QCD对称性的超子-核子势模型（如chiral EFT扩展）及中子星内部Λ/Λ̄丰度模拟提供关键基准。

Abstract: Using a sample of $(10087\pm44)\times10^{6}$ $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we investigate the inelastic scattering processes $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, exploiting hyperons from $J/ψ\toΛ\overlineΛ$ decays as an effective beam and the beam-pipe materials as targets. The processes $Λ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\toΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ and $\overlineΛ+{}^{9}\mathrm{Be}\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p+{}^{8}\mathrm{Li}$ are measured at a hyperon momentum of $1.074~\mathrm{GeV}/c$, with cross sections of $(10.1\pm1.4_{\rm stat}\pm0.7_{\rm syst})$ mb and $(1.7\pm0.6_{\rm stat}\pm0.4_{\rm syst})$ mb, respectively. Under the assumption of surface-dominated hyperon-nucleus scattering, these measurements are used to extract the corresponding proton-target cross sections. Independently, direct measurements using the hydrogen component of the beam-pipe oil yield $(3.2\pm1.1_{\rm stat}\pm0.5_{\rm syst})$ mb for $Λ+p\toΣ^{0}+p$ and $(1.5\pm0.5_{\rm stat}\pm0.1_{\rm syst})$ mb for $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$, consistent with the indirect determinations. The combined cross sections are $(4.7\pm0.7)$ mb and $(1.1\pm0.3)$ mb, respectively. The $\overlineΛ+p\to\overlineΣ^{0}+p$ signal constitutes the first evidence for anti-hyperon inelastic scattering with baryonic final states, with a significance of $3.1σ$. The pronounced difference between the $Λp$ and $\overlineΛp$ inelastic scattering cross sections provides new experimental constraints on hyperon-nucleon and anti-hyperon-nucleon interactions.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LZ实验利用2.84吨·年曝光量搜寻与氙核相互作用的暗物质，在扩展至270 keV的核反冲能窗中发现一个248 keV候选事例，其与纯背景假设存在2.6σ（全局）/3.4σ（局部）张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展传统自旋无关暗物质搜寻框架，覆盖有效场论（EFT）与非弹性散射等高能反冲主导模型，提升对弱耦合、大质量或非标准相互作用暗物质的灵敏度。

Method: 基于LZ探测器2.84 tonne-year曝光数据，采用扩展核反冲能谱分析（至270 keV），构建包含多种稀有本底拓扑（如中子、$^{85}$Kr/$^{222}$Rn衰变链、缪子诱导核反冲等）的精细化背景模型，并通过轮廓似然比检验评估信号显著性，计入遍历效应（look-elsewhere effect）。

Result: 观测到1个能量为248±23(stat)±23(sys) keV的核反冲候选事例，位于低本底区域；全局显著性2.6σ，局部最大显著性3.4σ，未达5σ发现阈值，但构成迄今最敏感的高能反冲暗物质限制之一。

Conclusion: 该事例不足以确立暗物质发现，但为EFT与非弹性暗物质模型设定了迄今最强的直接探测上限，并验证了LZ在高能核反冲区的探测能力与本底建模精度。

Abstract: We report on a search for dark matter particles interacting with xenon nuclei in an exposure of 2.84 tonne-years with the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment. An extended nuclear recoil energy window up to approximately 270 keV enables searches for effective field theory and inelastic models of dark matter where high-energy recoils account for a larger fraction of the predicted recoil spectrum compared to the nominal spin independent interaction. We observe one event with characteristics consistent with a nuclear recoil of $248\pm23\,\mathrm{(stat)}\pm23\,\mathrm{(sys)}$ keV, in a region where the known background expectation is low. A profile likelihood ratio test finds tension with the background-only hypothesis at a global significance of 2.6$σ$ when accounting for look-elsewhere effects, with a maximum local significance of 3.4$σ$ across the models tested. We describe the analysis and event of interest, the background model used in the statistical inference, and detail several of the rare background topologies considered.

</details>


### [90] [Effective Sub-Quantum Readout for Non-Monochromatic Axion Signals in High-$Q$ Haloscopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02828)
*Junu Jeong,Max Silva-Feaver*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种突破标准量子极限（SQL）的方法，利用约瑟夫森参量放大器（JPA）在微波腔轴子探测中实现0.5量子/频 bin 的有效噪声极限，通过将轴子信号对称地加载到信号与闲置带，并利用正交分析使信号功率加倍而真空噪声不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波腔轴子探测受限于标准量子极限（SQL），即相位保持线性放大引入的最小系统噪声为1量子/窄带信号，限制了弱轴子信号的灵敏度。

Method: 将约瑟夫森参量放大器工作于泵浦频率两倍处（即腔共振频率等于半泵浦频率），使轴子信号在信号带与闲置带对称激发；再通过外差读出的正交分量分析，实现两镜像谱分量的非相干叠加。

Result: 信号功率因双带非相干叠加而加倍，真空噪声保持不变，从而获得0.5量子/频 bin 的有效噪声；经最优匹配滤波后，整体等效噪声极限为1/√2量子。

Conclusion: 该方案突破传统SQL，为高Q腔轴子搜索提供了亚量子噪声探测新范式，适用于非单色轴子场（如宽谱或振荡质量轴子）的高灵敏探测。

Abstract: The search for wave-like dark matter using microwave cavity haloscopes is constrained by the Standard Quantum Limit, which dictates that phase-preserving linear amplification results in a minimum of one quantum of total system noise for a narrow-band signal. We demonstrate that this limit is effectively halved for a non-monochromatic axion signal coupled to a high-$Q$ cavity. By operating a Josephson Parametric Amplifier such that the cavity resonance is centered exactly at the half-pump frequency, the axion signal symmetrically populates both the signal and idler bands. Through quadrature analysis of the homodyne readout, we show that the incoherent sum of these mirrored spectral components doubles the measured signal power while the vacuum noise remains constant. This operation yields an effective noise limit of 0.5 quanta per frequency bin, translating to an overall effective limit of $1/\sqrt{2}$ quanta after optimal matched filtering.

</details>


### [91] [Sensitivity of an Early Dark Matter Search using the Electromagnetic Calorimeter as a Target for the Light Dark Matter eXperiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08121)
*LDMX Collaboration,Torsten Åkesson,Elizabeth Berzin,Cameron Bravo,Liam Brennan,Lene Kristian Bryngemark,Pierfrancesco Butti,Filippo Delzanno,E. Craig Dukes,Valentina Dutta,Bertrand Echenard,Ralf Ehrlich,Thomas Eichlersmith,Einar Elén,Andrew Furmanski,Victor Gomez,Matt Graham,Chiara Grieco,Craig Group,Hannah Herde,Christian Herwig,David G. Hitlin,Tyler Horoho,Joseph Incandela,Nathan Jay,Asahi Jige,Wesley Ketchum,Gordan Krnjaic,Amina Li,Zihan Ma,Jeremiah Mans,Cristina Mantilla Suarez,Sanjit Masanam,Phillip Masterson,Steven Metallo,Sophie Middleton,Joseph Muse,Timothy Nelson,Rory O'Dwyer,James Oyang,Jessica Pascadlo,Emrys Peets,Luis Sarmiento Pico,Ruth Pöttgen,Philip Schuster,Chris Sellgren,Lauren Tompkins,Natalia Toro,Nhan Tran,Tamas Vami,Erik Wallin,Yuxuan Wang,Andrew Whitbeck,Duncan Wilmot,Xinyi Xu,Danyi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了LDMX实验中利用电磁量能器作为活性靶的缺能搜寻方法，提升了对轻暗物质（1 MeV–10 MeV）的灵敏度，达到有效暗光子耦合强度 y ~ 2×10⁻¹³（1 MeV）至 5×10⁻¹²（10 MeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展LDMX对轻暗物质的探测能力，补充传统缺动量分析，尤其针对早期运行阶段提升低质量暗物质的探测覆盖。

Method: 将LDMX电磁量能器（ECal）重构为活性靶，构建基于有限变量的事件选择策略，实施缺能（missing-energy）分析，并进行蒙特卡洛模拟与背景估计。

Result: 在1 MeV和10 MeV暗物质质量下，分别实现y ≈ 2×10⁻¹³和y ≈ 5×10⁻¹²的耦合强度灵敏度，覆盖此前未被探测的轻暗物质参数空间。

Conclusion: 缺能分析可显著增强LDMX对亚GeV暗物质的探测能力，尤其适用于早期数据采集阶段，为暗光子媒介模型提供新约束。

Abstract: The Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX) is proposed to employ a thin tungsten target and a multi-GeV electron beam to carry out a missing momentum search for the production of dark matter candidate particles. We study the sensitivity for a complementary missing-energy-based search using the LDMX Electromagnetic Calorimeter as an active target with a focus on early running. In this context, we construct an event selection from a limited set of variables that projects sensitivity into previously-unexplored regions of light dark matter phase space -- down to an effective dark photon interaction strength $y$ of approximately $2\times10^{-13}$ ($5\times10^{-12}$) for a 1MeV (10MeV) dark matter candidate mass.

</details>
